# Agents · L · part 2

916 entries, learn-from-frustration to lighthouse-auditor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **learn-from-frustration**: Reads the recent conversation, the recent git diff, and the triggering event (user frustration message / code revert / verifier rejection / drift failure / audit pattern), then writes ONE structured feedback memory entry to .claude/memory/superdev-learned/. The entry contains the rule, why (with specific citations), and how-to-apply. Dispatched automatically by the UserPromptSubmit hook on frustration patterns and by SubagentStop after fix-applier / design-fidelity-auditor / regression-verifier / audit-synthesizer. One entry per dispatch — never bundles multiple lessons. ([boparaiamrit/superdev](https://github.com/boparaiamrit/superdev/blob/HEAD/plugins/superdev/agents/learn-from-frustration.md))
- **learn-learning-scientist**: Learning Scientist. Applies spacing, retrieval practice, flow-tuned difficulty, and ethical motivation (streaks, visible progress). Use on cold start for day sizing and after assessments when difficulty curve needs adjustment. ([VasiHemanth/learnloop](https://github.com/VasiHemanth/learnloop/blob/HEAD/agents/learning-scientist.md))
- **learn-notes**: Use right after finishing a piece of work to capture the reusable technique or gotcha worth remembering next time. ([Yazeed-Alotaibi/claude-config](https://github.com/Yazeed-Alotaibi/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/learn-notes.md))
- **learn-pace-analyst**: Pace Analyst. Reads daily logs and assessments to update pace model, detect drift, and decide if the horizon tail should be re-planned. Use after /learn done and when buffer is low. ([VasiHemanth/learnloop](https://github.com/VasiHemanth/learnloop/blob/HEAD/agents/pace-analyst.md))
- **learn-procedural-coach**: Design Phase 2 question ladders and Phase 3a summaries for /learn sessions on procedural topics (skill execution, debugging, problem-solving, computation). Single-dispatch per phase. Output is structured text the main agent executes interactively. ([honua-dmg/wayfarer](https://github.com/honua-dmg/wayfarer/blob/HEAD/agents/learn-procedural-coach.md))

Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: User wants to understand a concept. user: "Explain how async/await works in JavaScript" assistant: "I'll use the learning-guide agent to explain async/await with progressive examples and practical demonstrations." &lt;commentary&gt;Concept explanation requires learning-guide for pedagogical approach and clear examples.&lt;/commentary&gt;&lt;/exampl… ([samhvw8/dot-claude](https://github.com/samhvw8/dot-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/agents/learning-guide.md) · ★11 · 2 copies)

- **learning-judge**: Skeptical judgment gate for candidate learnings. Classifies each candidate as durable-learning, one-off, misunderstanding/spec-gap, or lisa-upstream with mandatory evidence citation. Hostile default — most candidates are DROPPED; only durable-learning ever persists. Use whenever a failure signal or debrief produces a claimed learning that something wants to write to the project learnings surface. ([CodySwannGT/lisa](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/lisa/blob/HEAD/plugins/lisa-agy/agents/learning-judge.md) · ★4 · 5 copies)
- **learning-loop**: Use proactively when Claude encounters any unfamiliar library, technology, or API not covered by existing skills. Self-learning pipeline: SEARCH existing skills, FETCH knowledge from trusted sources, EXECUTE task with attribution, VERIFY against knowledge, CREATE new skill. Triggers: code-skill-enforcer block, unknown import, new library, unfamiliar API, no dedicated skill exists. ([Alex1980Alex/1C-Framework](https://github.com/Alex1980Alex/1C-Framework/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/learning-loop.md))
- **learning-mentor**: Compatibility alias for the broader mentor agent. TRIGGER when a legacy caller explicitly invokes learning-mentor or asks for structured concept teaching, step-by-step tutorials, examples, pattern explanation, or learning feedback. - **librarian**: Understand and organize a body of information — files, notes, docs, datasets, a codebase's layout, a knowledge base. Builds and critiques taxonomies, says where something should live and where to look for it, identifies what should co-locate and what should split apart, and reduces entropy toward an organized, findable structure. Surveys and proposes a plan first; only moves or edits things on explicit approval. Trigger on intent — "organize this," "where should this go," "what's the right taxonomy," "where would I find X," "this is a mess, help me structure it." ([dpep/claude](https://github.com/dpep/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/personas/agents/librarian.md))
- **librarian**: Bridges Coscientist ↔ Zotero and manages citation-graph operations. - **librarian**: Audits a knowledge library and repairs what it finds — broken links, duplicate and fragmented topics, pages nothing links to, unsourced claims, index drift, unresolved contradictions. - **librarian**: Information-hygiene worker (the janitor). Proposes archival — never deletes. ([germs12/project-control-kit](https://github.com/germs12/project-control-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/librarian.md))
